HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001683/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0a0d629a52346aca7744","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:3:2\",\"root_001683\",[\"B005\"]],[\"112:3:4\",\"root_001683\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Doğumun iki yönde reddedilmesi, yalnız biyolojik doğumu değil, Allah'ın önceki bir kaynaktan türemesini veya kendisinden aynı düzeyde türemiş bir varlık çıkmasını da dışlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001683/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_179a67ebcaef2562c930","text":"1. **The paired verbs cancel both outgoing and incoming genealogy.** Lexical evidence: `و ل د B001` (born offspring), `و ل د B002` (parents by birth relation), `و ل د B003` (birth/delivery), plus 112:1 `ء ح د B001` and 112:4 `ك ف ء B001`. Structural cue: the same root appears twice, active then passive, each under `لم`; the sequence sits between `الصمد` and the final no-counterpart clause. Assigned roles: active `يلد` denies offspring proceeding from the subject; passive `يولد` denies parent-origin for the subject; `أحد/كفو` show why either direction would create comparable relational positions. Abductive move: I infer that the two negations close a genealogical circuit from both ends, not merely two independent facts. Reading change: 112:3 reads as a structural cancellation of lineage as an explanatory model for God.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001332/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3c1f0a3abd7056ae6723","text":"112:4 newly exposed a peer dimension inside the birth root: `ك ف ء:B001` plus exhaustive `ء ح د:B002` activated exploratory fixed `و ل د:B006`, so `لَمْ يُولَدْ` also resisted placement in a cohort of comparable born members. Its `ك و ن:B001` further sharpened fixed `و ل د:B005` by moving the denial from biological delivery toward derived occurrence in a temporal source-product chain.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001683/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5aa760e880d4079a408c","text":"112:4 made the peer branch `و ل د B006` more visible: the birth denial is not only about children and parents but about preventing a cohort from which a `كفو` could arise. Looking back to 112:0, the `ر ح م` branches produced a controlled tension: womb and post-birth imagery are activated, then refused as explanatory origin. Looking back to 112:2, `ص م د B002/B003` gave the double negation a material mechanism: no hollow, no generative opening, no derivative passage.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001683/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_72c368c6e3f32e2a198f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:3:2\",\"root_001683\",[\"B003\"]],[\"112:3:4\",\"root_001683\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Önceki dayanak niteliğinin doluluk ve tıkaç çağrışımı, burada doğumun dışarı ve içeri iki geçiş yolu gibi kapatılması biçiminde okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001683/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_743eb230d770d859dbe7","text":"4. **Peerhood by same origin is blocked before equivalence is named.** Lexical evidence: `و ل د B006` (same birth-age peer), `ء ح د B005` (individual isolation), and later `ك ف ء B001` (equal counterpart). Structural cue: birth is denied before `كفوًا أحد` is denied; the negation of origin precedes the negation of peer/equal. Assigned roles: `و ل د B006` supplies the latent social category of peers generated by shared birth-time or origin; `كفو` supplies the explicit counterpart category. Abductive move: I infer that counterparthood often rides on shared origin, generation, or cohort; the packet supplies the terms and order. Reading change: the ayah does preliminary work for 112:4: no birth means no cohort from which a peer or equal could emerge.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001683/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_82a150d972eee837fc3b","text":"1. **Birth relation closed in both directions.** Both fixed verbs are anchored in `و ل د:B003` (birth and delivery), but the first is active and the second passive, each independently governed by `لَمْ`. That reversal supplies opposed roles: issuing a birth and being the one brought forth. The repeated negation blocks each role rather than allowing one clause to be treated as a loose restatement of the other. I infer that the grammatical mirror is designed to exhaust the directionality of the same relation. The ayah therefore reads more strongly than simple childlessness: neither an outgoing parent role nor an incoming offspring origin can be assigned to the subject.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001683/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_85bdded97cd2ca463800","text":"2. **Generated derivation is denied, not only literal childbirth.** Lexical evidence: `و ل د B005` (generated or derived thing), `ق و ل B005` (false attribution), `ق و ل B013` (held doctrine), and `ص م د B002` (compact fullness). Structural cue: 112:1 commands a saying, 112:2 gives a dense predicate, then 112:3 negates `و ل د` in both directions. Assigned roles: `و ل د B005` expands the issue from biological child to anything derived/produced from something else; `ق و ل` frames this as a possible doctrine or attribution; `الصمد` supplies non-derived fullness. Abductive move: I infer that because the packet offers a non-biological branch for `و ل د`, the local denial reaches claims of generated essence, derivative status, or produced divinity. Reading change: the ayah rejects not only \"child\" language but any account in which the subject is a product or produces another entity of the same kind.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001683/B001","root_001683/B002","root_001683/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a12f3e2c3cb6470a83e1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:3:2\",\"root_001683\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B003\"]],[\"112:3:4\",\"root_001683\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Doğum kökü, açılıştaki rahim ve akrabalık yakınlığını yeniden görünür kılar; ardından bu yakınlığı ebeveyn veya çocuk olma ve soy statülerinden kesin biçimde ayırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001683/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ba0bba46a6f143d916ae","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:3:2\",\"root_001683\",[\"B006\"]],[\"112:3:4\",\"root_001683\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Doğurulmamış olmak, Allah'ı ortak köken veya yaştan doğan bir akranlar kuşağına yerleştirmeyi de engeller.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bdc9c1371cf64e1fbf07","text":"3. **Mercy/womb imagery is retained but not allowed to become origin.** Lexical evidence: prior `ر ح م B003` (womb), `ر ح م B004` (post-birth womb distress), `ر ح م B001` (mercy), and present `و ل د B003`. Structural cue: the basmalah's two mercy adjectives precede the surah; the only explicit birth-root later is completely negated. Assigned roles: `ر ح م` supplies the dormant bodily matrix; `لم يلد ولم يولد` prevents that matrix from becoming a literal generative explanation. Abductive move: I infer backward activation: the birth negation forces a distinction between mercy as intimate care and womb as biological origin. Reading change: 112:3 becomes a guardrail for the basmalah: divine mercy can sound near and womb-like without implying that God participates in birth.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001683/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bef3f969273357b57a5c","text":"3. **Family intimacy is separated from family status.** Fixed-ayah `و ل د:B001` and `و ل د:B002` supply the paired statuses of offspring and parents. Earlier, the two mercy forms activate `ر ح م:B002` (kinship) and `ر ح م:B003` (womb), so the packet first makes a family-shaped field lexically available and then negates its defining birth relation here. Offspring and parent branches name the social slots being removed; the mercy root preserves an analogy of intimate care without filling those slots. I infer that the explicit birth negation corrects, rather than erases, the earlier family imagery. The fixed ayah thus reads as a boundary between compassionate nearness and genealogical membership.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001683/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e768c396f3dc36b824f2","text":"2. **No source-product derivation, not only no biological delivery.** The fixed root's `و ل د:B005` covers something generated, derived, or newly produced from something else. Context activates that dormant scope: 112:2's `ص م د:B002` supplies compact integrity, and 112:4's `ك و ن:B001` supplies occurrence in time. In the active/passive mirror, the derived-thing branch models both being a producer of a same-order derivative and being a product whose existence derives from a prior source. The abductive broadening from birth to derivation is licensed by the accepted branch but is not forced by the verb forms alone. On this reading, the ayah denies causal or ontological production in either direction in addition to literal genealogy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001683/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f203035ed4276fa4292d","text":"4. **No placement in a generational peer class.** Exploratorily, fixed-root `و ل د:B006` carries a same-birth-age peer, although that branch is normally realized through a different derivative than the two verbs here. Birth ordinarily locates someone within a temporal cohort; later `ك ف ء:B001` denies an equal counterpart and final `ء ح د:B002` exhausts anyone who might fill that role. The peer branch gives birth a cohort-locating function, while the final clause removes every comparable member. I infer the bridge from being born to possessing coeval peers, so this remains weaker than the direct birth reading. It nevertheless changes `لَمْ يُولَدْ` into a denial of placement within a generation whose members could be compared by common origin or age.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
